# Claim ledger: audit vectors-1-3-r2

Course `vectors` (Stage 1, full ledger), levels 1 to 3, at commit 461a68d2. One row per claim-bearing line: prose, caption, graded key, slip value, option `why`, hint, feedback line, explain, summary line, model paragraph, rubric line, derivation line, hidden-test set and mutant. The rows are listed by a script from the content files; each verdict comes from the auditor reading that line, and each figure in it was recomputed (by hand during the read, and for the keys and slips named in the basis by `docs/reports/vectors-L1-3-concepts-r2.py`). The basis column gives the source for the screen or file, a derivation, or the simulation or run that checks the claim. "concepts-r2 [x]" is the check tagged x in that script; "sweep" is `docs/reports/vectors-L1-3-concepts-r2-sweep.mjs` under Pyodide 0.27.7.

Rows: 3002. Verdicts: correct 2983, wrong 1, imprecise 14, misleading 4, unverified 0.
